</div></td></tr><tr><th scope="row" class="infobox-label">Religion <div class="ib-country-religion"><sup id="cite_ref-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-6">&#91;4&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-7" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-7">&#91;5&#93;</a></sup></div></th><td class="infobox-data"><a href="/wiki/Christianity" title="Christianity">Christianity</a> (<a href="/wiki/Catholicism" class="mw-redirect" title="Catholicism">Catholicism</a>)</td></tr><tr><th scope="row" class="infobox-label"><a href="/wiki/Demonym" title="Demonym">Demonym(s)</a></th><td class="infobox-data"><a href="/wiki/List_of_Andorrans" title="List of Andorrans">Andorran</a></td></tr><tr><th scope="row" class="infobox-label"><a href="/wiki/Politics_of_Andorra" title="Politics of Andorra">Government</a></th><td class="infobox-data"><span class="nowrap"><a href="/wiki/Unitary_state" title="Unitary state">Unitary</a> <a href="/wiki/Parliamentary_system" title="Parliamentary system">parliamentary</a></span> <a href="/wiki/Constitutional_monarchy" title="Constitutional monarchy">constitutional</a> <a href="/wiki/Elective_monarchy" title="Elective monarchy">elective</a> <a href="/wiki/Diarchy" title="Diarchy">diarchy</a></td></tr><tr class="mergedrow"><td colspan="2" class="infobox-full-data"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1066479718"/></td></tr><tr class="mergedrow"><th scope="row" class="infobox-label"><div style="text-indent:-0.9em;margin-left:1.2em;font-weight:normal;">•&#160;<a href="/wiki/Co-Princes_of_Andorra" title="Co-Princes of Andorra">Co-Princes</a> </div></th><td class="infobox-data"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1126788409"/><div class="plainlist">
