.trow{justify-content:center}.mw-parser-output .tmulti .tsingle{float:none!important;max-width:100%!important;box-sizing:border-box;text-align:center}.mw-parser-output .tmulti .tsingle .thumbcaption{text-align:left}.mw-parser-output .tmulti .trow>.thumbcaption{text-align:center}}</style><div class="thumb tmulti tleft"><div class="thumbinner multiimageinner" style="width:290px;max-width:290px"><div class="trow"><div class="tsingle" style="width:152px;max-width:152px"><div class="thumbimage"><a href="/wiki/File:Mons._Vives_(30612833490).jpg" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/30/Mons._Vives_%2830612833490%29.jpg/150px-Mons._Vives_%2830612833490%29.jpg" decoding="async" width="150" height="189" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/30/Mons._Vives_%2830612833490%29.jpg/225px-Mons._Vives_%2830612833490%29.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/30/Mons._Vives_%2830612833490%29.jpg/300px-Mons._Vives_%2830612833490%29.jpg 2x" data-file-width="600" data-file-height="755" /></a></div><div class="thumbcaption"><b><a href="/wiki/Joan_Enric_Vives_i_Sic%C3%ADlia" title="Joan Enric Vives i Sicília">Joan Enric Vives i Sicília</a></b><br />&#160;&#8211;&#32; <a href="/wiki/Bishop_of_Urgell" class="mw-redirect" title="Bishop of Urgell">Bishop of Urgell</a> since 12 May 2003</div></div><div class="tsingle" style="width:134px;max-width:134px"><div class="thumbimage"><a href="/wiki/File:Emmanuel_Macron_(cropped).jpg" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/c3/Emmanuel_Macron_%28cropped%29.jpg/132px-Emmanuel_Macron_%28cropped%29.jpg" decoding="async" width="132" height="189" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/c3/Emmanuel_Macron_%28cropped%29.jpg/198px-Emmanuel_Macron_%28cropped%29.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/c3/Emmanuel_Macron_%28cropped%29.jpg/264px-Emmanuel_Macron_%28cropped%29.jpg 2x" data-file-width="521" data-file-height="745" /></a></div><div class="thumbcaption"><b><a href="/wiki/Emmanuel_Macron" title="Emmanuel Macron">Emmanuel Macron</a></b><br />&#160;&#8211;&#32; <a href="/wiki/President_of_France" title="President of France">President of France</a> since 14 May 2017</div></div></div></div></div>
<p>Andorra is a parliamentary <a href="/wiki/Co-principality" class="mw-redirect" title="Co-principality">co-principality</a> with the <a href="/wiki/Bishop_of_Urgell" class="mw-redirect" title="Bishop of Urgell">Bishop of Urgell</a> and the president of France as <a href="/wiki/List_of_Co-Princes_of_Andorra" class="mw-redirect" title="List of Co-Princes of Andorra">co-princes</a>. This peculiarity makes the president of France, in his capacity as <a href="/wiki/Prince_of_Andorra" class="mw-redirect" title="Prince of Andorra">prince of Andorra</a>, an elected monarch, although he is not elected by a popular vote of the Andorran people. The politics of Andorra take place in a framework of a <a href="/wiki/Parliamentary_system" title="Parliamentary system">parliamentary</a> <a href="/wiki/Representative_democracy" title="Representative democracy">representative democracy</a> with a <a href="/wiki/Unicameral" class="mw-redirect" title="Unicameral">unicameral</a> legislature, and of a <a href="https://en.wiktionary.org/wiki/pluriform" class="extiw" title="wikt:pluriform">pluriform</a> multi-party system. The <a href="/wiki/List_of_heads_of_government_of_Andorra" title="List of heads of government of Andorra">prime minister</a> is the <a href="/wiki/Head_of_government" title="Head of government">chief executive</a>.
</p><p>The current Prime Minister is <a href="/wiki/Xavier_Espot_Zamora" title="Xavier Espot Zamora">Xavier Espot Zamora</a> of the <a href="/wiki/Democrats_for_Andorra" title="Democrats for Andorra">Democrats for Andorra</a> (DA). <a href="/wiki/Executive_power" class="mw-redirect" title="Executive power">Executive power</a> is exercised by the government. Legislative power is vested in both government and parliament.
</p><p>The Parliament of Andorra is known as the <a href="/wiki/General_Council_(Andorra)" title="General Council (Andorra)">General Council</a>. The General Council consists of between 28 and 42 councillors. The councillors serve for four-year terms, and elections are held between the 30th and 40th days following the dissolution of the previous Council.
</p><p>Half are elected in equal numbers by each of the seven administrative parishes, and the other half of the councillors are elected in a single national constituency. Fifteen days after the election, the councillors hold their inauguration. During this session, the <a href="/wiki/List_of_general_syndics_of_the_General_Council" title="List of general syndics of the General Council">General Syndic</a>, who is the head of the General Council, and the Subsyndic General, his assistant, are elected. Eight days later, the Council convenes once more. During this session, the prime minister is chosen from among the councillors.
</p>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:222px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Casa_de_la_Vall_-_18.jpg" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/8e/Casa_de_la_Vall_-_18.jpg/220px-Casa_de_la_Vall_-_18.jpg" decoding="async" width="220" height="137" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/8e/Casa_de_la_Vall_-_18.jpg/330px-Casa_de_la_Vall_-_18.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/8e/Casa_de_la_Vall_-_18.jpg/440px-Casa_de_la_Vall_-_18.jpg 2x" data-file-width="3560" data-file-height="2222" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Casa_de_la_Vall_-_18.jpg" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>Casa de la Vall, the historical and ceremonial Andorran Parliament</div></div></div>
<p>Candidates can be proposed by a minimum of one-fifth of the councillors. The Council then elects the candidate with the absolute majority of votes to be prime minister. The Syndic General then notifies the co-princes, who in turn appoint the elected candidate as the prime minister of Andorra. The General Council is also responsible for proposing and passing laws. Bills may be presented to the council as Private Members' Bills by three of the local Parish Councils jointly or by at least one tenth of the citizens of Andorra.
</p>
<div class="thumb tleft"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:222px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Casa_de_la_Vall,_Andorra_la_Vieja,_Andorra,_2013-12-30,_DD_03.JPG" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/49/Casa_de_la_Vall%2C_Andorra_la_Vieja%2C_Andorra%2C_2013-12-30%2C_DD_03.JPG/220px-Casa_de_la_Vall%2C_Andorra_la_Vieja%2C_Andorra%2C_2013-12-30%2C_DD_03.JPG" decoding="async" width="220" height="78" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/49/Casa_de_la_Vall%2C_Andorra_la_Vieja%2C_Andorra%2C_2013-12-30%2C_DD_03.JPG/330px-Casa_de_la_Vall%2C_Andorra_la_Vieja%2C_Andorra%2C_2013-12-30%2C_DD_03.JPG 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/49/Casa_de_la_Vall%2C_Andorra_la_Vieja%2C_Andorra%2C_2013-12-30%2C_DD_03.JPG/440px-Casa_de_la_Vall%2C_Andorra_la_Vieja%2C_Andorra%2C_2013-12-30%2C_DD_03.JPG 2x" data-file-width="5426" data-file-height="1933" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Casa_de_la_Vall,_Andorra_la_Vieja,_Andorra,_2013-12-30,_DD_03.JPG" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>The <a href="/wiki/New_Parliament_of_Andorra" title="New Parliament of Andorra">New Parliament of Andorra</a>, headquarters of the <a href="/wiki/General_Council_of_Andorra" class="mw-redirect" title="General Council of Andorra">General Council</a> since 2011, next to <a href="/wiki/Casa_de_la_Vall" title="Casa de la Vall">Casa de la Vall</a></div></div></div>
<p>The council also approves the annual budget of the principality. The government must submit the proposed budget for parliamentary approval at least two months before the previous budget expires. If the budget is not approved by the first day of the next year, the previous budget is extended until a new one is approved. Once any bill is approved, the Syndic General is responsible for presenting it to the co-princes so that they may sign and enact it.
</p>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:222px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Els_7_poetes,_Casa_de_la_Vall,_Consell_General.JPG" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/7b/Els_7_poetes%2C_Casa_de_la_Vall%2C_Consell_General.JPG/220px-Els_7_poetes%2C_Casa_de_la_Vall%2C_Consell_General.JPG" decoding="async" width="220" height="165" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/7b/Els_7_poetes%2C_Casa_de_la_Vall%2C_Consell_General.JPG/330px-Els_7_poetes%2C_Casa_de_la_Vall%2C_Consell_General.JPG 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/7b/Els_7_poetes%2C_Casa_de_la_Vall%2C_Consell_General.JPG/440px-Els_7_poetes%2C_Casa_de_la_Vall%2C_Consell_General.JPG 2x" data-file-width="3264" data-file-height="2448" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Els_7_poetes,_Casa_de_la_Vall,_Consell_General.JPG" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>The centre of government in <a href="/wiki/Andorra_la_Vella" title="Andorra la Vella">Andorra la Vella</a></div></div></div>
<p>If the prime minister is not satisfied with the council, he may request that the co-princes dissolve the council and order new elections. In turn, the councillors have the power to remove the prime minister from office. After a motion of censure is approved by at least one-fifth of the councillors, the council will vote and if it receives the absolute majority of votes, the prime minister is removed.
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Law_and_criminal_justice">Law and criminal justice</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Andorra&amp;action=edit&amp;section=11" title="Edit section: Law and criminal justice">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"/><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/Law_of_Andorra" title="Law of Andorra">Law of Andorra</a></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1097763485"/><table class="box-Unreferenced_section plainlinks metadata ambox ambox-content ambox-Unreferenced" role="presentation"><tbody><tr><td class="mbox-image"><div class="mbox-image-div"><a href="/wiki/File:Question_book-new.svg" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/50px-Question_book-new.svg.png" decoding="async" width="50" height="39" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/75px-Question_book-new.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/100px-Question_book-new.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="399" /></a></div></td><td class="mbox-text"><div class="mbox-text-span">This section <b>does not <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citing_sources" title="Wikipedia:Citing sources">cite</a> any <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">sources</a></b>.<span class="hide-when-compact"> Please help <a class="external text" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Andorra&amp;action=edit">improve this section</a> by <a href="/wiki/Help:Referencing_for_beginners" title="Help:Referencing for beginners">adding citations to reliable sources</a>. Unsourced material may be challenged and <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability#Burden_of_evidence" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">removed</a>.</span>  <span class="date-container"><i>(<span class="date">December 2021</span>)</i></span><span class="hide-when-compact"><i> (<small><a href="/wiki/Help:Maintenance_template_removal" title="Help:Maintenance template removal">Learn how and when to remove this template message</a></small>)</i></span></div></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>The judiciary is composed of the Magistrates Court, the Criminal Law Court, the High Court of Andorra, and the Constitutional Court. The High Court of Justice is composed of five judges: one appointed by the prime minister, one each by the co-princes, one by the Syndic General, and one by the judges and magistrates. It is presided over by the member appointed by the Syndic General and the judges hold office for six-year terms.
</p><p>The magistrates and judges are appointed by the High Court, as is the president of the Criminal Law Court. The High Court also appoints members of the Office of the Attorney General. The Constitutional Court is responsible for interpreting the Constitution and reviewing all appeals of unconstitutionality against laws and treaties. It is composed of four judges, one appointed by each of the co-princes and two by the General Council. They serve eight-year terms. The Court is presided over by one of the judges on a two-year rotation so that each judge at one point will preside over the Court.
</p>
<h2><span id="Foreign_relations.2C_defence_and_security"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Foreign_relations,_defence_and_security">Foreign relations, defence and security</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Andorra&amp;action=edit&amp;section=12" title="Edit section: Foreign relations, defence and security">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"/><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/Foreign_relations_of_Andorra" title="Foreign relations of Andorra">Foreign relations of Andorra</a></div>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:222px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Bruxelles_rue_de_la_Montagne_10.jpg" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/39/Bruxelles_rue_de_la_Montagne_10.jpg/220px-Bruxelles_rue_de_la_Montagne_10.jpg" decoding="async" width="220" height="330" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/39/Bruxelles_rue_de_la_Montagne_10.jpg/330px-Bruxelles_rue_de_la_Montagne_10.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/39/Bruxelles_rue_de_la_Montagne_10.jpg/440px-Bruxelles_rue_de_la_Montagne_10.jpg 2x" data-file-width="2592" data-file-height="3888" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Bruxelles_rue_de_la_Montagne_10.jpg" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>The embassy of Andorra in <a href="/wiki/Brussels" title="Brussels">Brussels</a></div></div></div>
<p>Andorra does not have its own armed forces,<sup id="cite_ref-cia_5-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-cia-5">&#91;3&#93;</a></sup> although there is a small ceremonial army. Responsibility for defending the nation rests primarily with France and Spain.<sup id="cite_ref-145" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-145">&#91;136&#93;</a></sup> However, in case of emergencies or natural disasters, the Sometent (an alarm) is called and all able-bodied men between 21 and 60 of Andorran nationality must serve.<sup id="cite_ref-146" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-146">&#91;137&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-recarrega.net_147-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-recarrega.net-147">&#91;138&#93;</a></sup> This is why all Andorrans, and especially the head of each house (usually the eldest able-bodied man of a house) should, by law, keep a rifle, even though the law also states that the police will offer a firearm in case of need.<sup id="cite_ref-recarrega.net_147-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-recarrega.net-147">&#91;138&#93;</a></sup> Andorra is a full member of the United Nations (UN), the <a href="/wiki/Organization_for_Security_and_Co-operation_in_Europe" title="Organization for Security and Co-operation in Europe">Organization for Security and Co-operation in Europe</a> (OSCE), and has a special agreement with the European Union (EU), it also has observer status at the <a href="/wiki/World_Trade_Organization" title="World Trade Organization">World Trade Organization</a> (WTO).<sup id="cite_ref-148" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-148">&#91;139&#93;</a></sup> On 16 October 2020, Andorra became the 190th member of the <a href="/wiki/International_Monetary_Fund" title="International Monetary Fund">International Monetary Fund</a> (IMF), during the <a href="/wiki/COVID-19_pandemic" title="COVID-19 pandemic">COVID-19 pandemic</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-149" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-149">&#91;140&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-150" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-150">&#91;141&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Military">Military</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Andorra&amp;action=edit&amp;section=13" title="Edit section: Military">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>Andorra has a small army, which has historically been raised or reconstituted at various dates, but has never in modern times amounted to a standing army. The basic principle of Andorran defence is that all able-bodied men are available to fight if called upon by the summoning of the Sometent (a civil defense organization of Andorra, made up of the heads of households). Being a <a href="/wiki/Landlocked_country" title="Landlocked country">landlocked country</a>, Andorra has no navy.
</p><p>Before <a href="/wiki/World_War_I" title="World War I">World War I</a>, Andorra maintained an armed <a href="/wiki/Militia" title="Militia">militia</a> force of about 600 part-time militiamen under the supervision of a Captain (Capità or Cap de Sometent) and a Lieutenant (Desener or Lloctinent del Capità). This body was not liable for service outside the principality and was commanded by two officials (veguers) appointed by France and the Bishop of Urgell.<sup id="cite_ref-151" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-151">&#91;142&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>In the modern era, the army has consisted of a very small body of volunteers willing to undertake <a href="/wiki/Honor_guard" class="mw-redirect" title="Honor guard">ceremonial duties</a>. Uniforms and weaponry were handed down from generation to generation within families and communities.<sup id="cite_ref-elperiodic.ad_152-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-elperiodic.ad-152">&#91;143&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>The army's role in internal security was largely taken over by the formation of the <a href="/wiki/Police_Corps_of_Andorra" title="Police Corps of Andorra">Police Corps of Andorra</a> in 1931. Brief civil disorder associated with the elections of 1933 led to assistance being sought from the French <a href="/wiki/National_Gendarmerie" title="National Gendarmerie">National Gendarmerie</a>,<sup id="cite_ref-153" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-153">&#91;144&#93;</a></sup> with a detachment resident in Andorra for two months under the command of René-Jules Baulard.<sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;">&#91;<i><a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (August 2020)">citation needed</span></a></i>&#93;</sup> The Andorran Police was reformed in the following year, with eleven soldiers appointed to supervisory roles.<sup id="cite_ref-154" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-154">&#91;145&#93;</a></sup> The force consisted of six <a href="/wiki/Corporal" title="Corporal">Corporals</a>, one for each parish (although there are currently seven parishes, there were only six until 1978), plus four junior staff officers to co-ordinate action, and a commander with the rank of major. It was the responsibility of the six corporals, each in his own parish, to be able to raise a fighting force from among the able-bodied men of the parish.
</p><p>The only permanent section of the present-day Sometent is a twelve-man ceremonial unit. However, all able-bodied men are technically <a href="/wiki/Conscription" title="Conscription">available for military service</a>,<sup id="cite_ref-155" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-155">&#91;146&#93;</a></sup> with a requirement for each family to have access to a firearm. An <a href="/wiki/Shot_(pellet)" title="Shot (pellet)">area weapon</a> such as a Shotgun per household is unregulated, however <a href="/wiki/Bullet" title="Bullet">ranged weapons</a> such as Pistols and Rifles require a license.<sup id="cite_ref-elperiodic.ad_152-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-elperiodic.ad-152">&#91;143&#93;</a></sup> The army has not fought for more than 700 years, and its main responsibility is to present the <a href="/wiki/Flag_of_Andorra" title="Flag of Andorra">flag of Andorra</a> at official ceremonial functions.<sup id="cite_ref-156" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-156">&#91;147&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-157" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-157">&#91;148&#93;</a></sup> According to <a href="/wiki/Marc_Forn%C3%A9_Moln%C3%A9" title="Marc Forné Molné">Marc Forné Molné</a>, Andorra's military budget is strictly from voluntary donations, and the availability of full-time volunteers.<sup id="cite_ref-158" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-158">&#91;149&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>In more recent times there has only been a general emergency call to the popular army of Sometent during the floods of 1982 in the Catalan Pyrenees,<sup id="cite_ref-159" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-159">&#91;150&#93;</a></sup> where 12 citizens perished in Andorra, to help the population and establish a public order along with the Local Police units.<sup id="cite_ref-160" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-160">&#91;151&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Police_Corps">Police Corps</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Andorra&amp;action=edit&amp;section=14" title="Edit section: Police Corps">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:222px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Skoda_Policia_-_Andorra_(2022).jpg" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/9/9d/Skoda_Policia_-_Andorra_%282022%29.jpg/220px-Skoda_Policia_-_Andorra_%282022%29.jpg" decoding="async" width="220" height="165" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/9/9d/Skoda_Policia_-_Andorra_%282022%29.jpg/330px-Skoda_Policia_-_Andorra_%282022%29.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/9/9d/Skoda_Policia_-_Andorra_%282022%29.jpg/440px-Skoda_Policia_-_Andorra_%282022%29.jpg 2x" data-file-width="4000" data-file-height="3000" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Skoda_Policia_-_Andorra_(2022).jpg" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div><a href="/wiki/Police_Corps_of_Andorra" title="Police Corps of Andorra">National Police</a> patrolling the central area of the capital city</div></div></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"/><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/Police_Corps_of_Andorra" title="Police Corps of Andorra">Police Corps of Andorra</a></div>
<p>Andorra maintains a small but modern and well-equipped internal police force, with around 240 police officers supported by civilian assistants. The principal services supplied by the corps are uniformed community policing, criminal detection, border control, and traffic policing. There are also small specialist units including police dogs, mountain rescue, and a bomb disposal team.<sup id="cite_ref-161" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-161">&#91;152&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="GIPA">GIPA</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Andorra&amp;action=edit&amp;section=15" title="Edit section: GIPA">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>The <i>Grup d'Intervenció Policia d'Andorra</i> (GIPA) is a small <a href="/wiki/Police_tactical_unit" title="Police tactical unit">special forces squad</a> trained in <a href="/wiki/Counter-terrorism" class="mw-redirect" title="Counter-terrorism">counter-terrorism</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Hostage_negotiator" class="mw-redirect" title="Hostage negotiator">hostage recovery</a> tasks. Although it is the <a href="/wiki/Paramilitary" title="Paramilitary">closest in style to an active military force</a>, it is part of the <a href="/wiki/Police_Corps_of_Andorra" title="Police Corps of Andorra">Police Corps</a>, and not the army. As terrorist and hostage situations are a rare threat to the country, the GIPA is commonly assigned to prisoner escort duties, and at other times to routine policing.<sup id="cite_ref-162" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-162">&#91;153&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Fire_brigade">Fire brigade</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Andorra&amp;action=edit&amp;section=16" title="Edit section: Fire brigade">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>The Andorran Fire Brigade, with headquarters at <a href="/wiki/Santa_Coloma_d%27Andorra" title="Santa Coloma d&#39;Andorra">Santa Coloma</a>, operates from four modern fire stations, and has a staff of around 120 firefighters. The service is equipped with 16 heavy appliances (fire tenders, turntable ladders, and specialist four-wheel drive vehicles), four light support vehicles (cars and vans) and four ambulances.<sup id="cite_ref-163" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-163">&#91;154&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>Historically, the families of the six ancient parishes of Andorra maintained local arrangements to assist each other in fighting fires. The first fire pump purchased by the government was acquired in 1943. Serious fires which lasted for two days in December 1959 led to calls for a permanent fire service, and the Andorran Fire Brigade was formed on 21 April 1961.<sup id="cite_ref-164" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-164">&#91;155&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>The fire service maintains full-time cover with five fire crews on duty at any time: two at the brigade's headquarters in Santa Coloma, and one crew at each of the other three fire stations.<sup id="cite_ref-165" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-165">&#91;156&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Geography">Geography</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Andorra&amp;action=edit&amp;section=17" title="Edit section: Geography">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:222px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Andorramap.png" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/ef/Andorramap.png/220px-Andorramap.png" decoding="async" width="220" height="179" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/ef/Andorramap.png/330px-Andorramap.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/ef/Andorramap.png/440px-Andorramap.png 2x" data-file-width="800" data-file-height="650" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Andorramap.png" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>Map of Andorra with its seven parishes labelled</div></div></div>

<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"/><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main articles: <a href="/wiki/Geography_of_Andorra" title="Geography of Andorra">Geography of Andorra</a> and <a href="/wiki/Geology_of_Andorra" title="Geology of Andorra">Geology of Andorra</a></div>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Parishes">Parishes</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Andorra&amp;action=edit&amp;section=18" title="Edit section: Parishes">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"/><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/Parishes_of_Andorra" title="Parishes of Andorra">Parishes of Andorra</a></div>
<p>Andorra consists of seven parishes:
</p>

<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Physical_geography">Physical geography</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Andorra&amp;action=edit&amp;section=19" title="Edit section: Physical geography">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>Due to its location in the eastern <a href="/wiki/Pyrenees" title="Pyrenees">Pyrenees</a> mountain range, Andorra consists predominantly of rugged mountains, the highest being the <a href="/wiki/Coma_Pedrosa" title="Coma Pedrosa">Coma Pedrosa</a> at 2,942 metres (9,652&#160;ft), and the average elevation of Andorra is 1,996 metres (6,549&#160;ft).<sup id="cite_ref-166" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-166">&#91;157&#93;</a></sup> These are dissected by three narrow valleys in a Y shape that combine into one as the main stream, the <a href="/wiki/Gran_Valira" title="Gran Valira">Gran Valira</a> river, leaves the country for Spain (at Andorra's lowest point of 840&#160;m or 2,756&#160;ft). Andorra's land area is 468&#160;km<sup>2</sup> (181&#160;sq&#160;mi).
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Environment">Environment</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Andorra&amp;action=edit&amp;section=20" title="Edit section: Environment">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p><a href="/wiki/Phytogeography" title="Phytogeography">Phytogeographically</a>, Andorra belongs to the Atlantic European province of the <a href="/wiki/Circumboreal_Region" title="Circumboreal Region">Circumboreal Region</a> within the <a href="/wiki/Boreal_Kingdom" title="Boreal Kingdom">Boreal Kingdom</a>. According to the <a href="/wiki/World_Wide_Fund_for_Nature" title="World Wide Fund for Nature">WWF</a>, the territory of Andorra belongs to the <a href="/wiki/Ecoregion" title="Ecoregion">ecoregion</a> of <a href="/wiki/Pyrenees_conifer_and_mixed_forests" title="Pyrenees conifer and mixed forests">Pyrenees conifer and mixed forests</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-DinersteinOlson2017_167-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-DinersteinOlson2017-167">&#91;158&#93;</a></sup> Andorra had a 2018 <a href="/wiki/Forest_Landscape_Integrity_Index" title="Forest Landscape Integrity Index">Forest Landscape Integrity Index</a> mean score of 4.45/10, ranking it 127th globally out of 172 countries.<sup id="cite_ref-FLII-Supplementary_168-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-FLII-Supplementary-168">&#91;159&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Important_Bird_Area">Important Bird Area</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Andorra&amp;action=edit&amp;section=21" title="Edit section: Important Bird Area">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>The whole country has been recognised as a single <a href="/wiki/Important_Bird_Area" title="Important Bird Area">Important Bird Area</a> (IBA) by <a href="/wiki/BirdLife_International" title="BirdLife International">BirdLife International</a>, because it is important for forest and mountain birds and supports populations of <a href="/wiki/Red-billed_chough" title="Red-billed chough">red-billed choughs</a>, <a href="/wiki/Citril_finch" title="Citril finch">citril finches</a> and <a href="/wiki/Rock_bunting" title="Rock bunting">rock buntings</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-bli_169-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-bli-169">&#91;160&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Climate">Climate</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Andorra&amp;action=edit&amp;section=22" title="Edit section: Climate">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>Andorra has <a href="/wiki/Alpine_climate" title="Alpine climate">alpine</a>, <a href="/wiki/Continental_climate" title="Continental climate">continental</a> and <a href="/wiki/Oceanic_climate" title="Oceanic climate">oceanic climates</a>, depending on altitude. Its higher elevation means there is, on average, more snow in winter and it is slightly cooler in summer. The diversity of landmarks, the different orientation of the valleys and the irregularity relief typical of the <a href="/wiki/Mediterranean_climate" title="Mediterranean climate">Mediterranean climates</a> make the country have a great diversity of <a href="/wiki/Microclimate" title="Microclimate">microclimates</a> that hinder the general dominance of the <a href="/wiki/Mountain_climate" class="mw-redirect" title="Mountain climate">high mountain climate</a>. The great differences of altitude in the minimum and maximum points, together with the influence of a Mediterranean climate, develop the climate of the Andorran Pyrenees.
</p><p>When in precipitation, a global model characterized by convective and abundant rains can be defined during spring and summer, which can last until autumn (May, June and August are usually the rainiest months). In winter, however, it is less rainy, except in the highlands, subject to the influence of fronts from the <a href="/wiki/Atlantic_Europe" title="Atlantic Europe">Atlantic</a>, which explains the great amount of snowfall in the Andorran mountains. The temperature regime is characterized, broadly, by a temperate summer and a long and cold winter, in accordance with the mountainous condition of the Principality.<sup id="cite_ref-170" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-170">&#91;161&#93;</a></sup>
</p>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Economy">Economy</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Andorra&amp;action=edit&amp;section=23" title="Edit section: Economy">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"/><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/Economy_of_Andorra" title="Economy of Andorra">Economy of Andorra</a></div>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:312px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Andorra_Product_Exports_(2019).svg" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/86/Andorra_Product_Exports_%282019%29.svg/310px-Andorra_Product_Exports_%282019%29.svg.png" decoding="async" width="310" height="202" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/86/Andorra_Product_Exports_%282019%29.svg/465px-Andorra_Product_Exports_%282019%29.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/86/Andorra_Product_Exports_%282019%29.svg/620px-Andorra_Product_Exports_%282019%29.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="1100" data-file-height="715" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Andorra_Product_Exports_(2019).svg" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>A proportional representation of Andorra exports, 2019</div></div></div>
<p>Tourism, the mainstay of Andorra's tiny, well-to-do economy, accounts for roughly 80% of GDP. An estimated 10.2&#160;million tourists visit annually,<sup id="cite_ref-es2_29-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-es2-29">&#91;21&#93;</a></sup> attracted by Andorra's <a href="/wiki/Duty-free_shopping" class="mw-redirect" title="Duty-free shopping">duty-free</a> status and by its summer and winter resorts.
</p><p>One of the main sources of income in Andorra is tourism from ski resorts which total over 175&#160;km (109&#160;mi) of ski ground. The sport brings in over 7 million visitors annually and an estimated 340 million euros per year, sustaining 2,000 direct and 10,000 indirect jobs at present since 2007.<sup id="cite_ref-173" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-173">&#91;164&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>The banking sector, with its <a href="/wiki/Tax_haven" title="Tax haven">tax haven</a> status, also contributes substantially to the economy with revenues raised exclusively through import tariffs (the financial and insurance sector accounts for approximately 19% of GDP<sup id="cite_ref-fs2013_174-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-fs2013-174">&#91;165&#93;</a></sup>). However, during the <a href="/wiki/European_sovereign-debt_crisis" class="mw-redirect" title="European sovereign-debt crisis">European sovereign-debt crisis</a> of the 21st century, the tourist industry suffered a decline, partly caused by a drop in the prices of goods in Spain, undercutting <a href="/wiki/Duty-free_shop" title="Duty-free shop">duty-free shopping</a> and increasing unemployment. On 1 January 2012, a business tax of 10% was introduced,<sup id="cite_ref-175" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-175">&#91;166&#93;</a></sup> followed by a sales tax of 2% a year later, which raised just over 14 million euros in its first quarter.<sup id="cite_ref-176" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-176">&#91;167&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>Agricultural production is limited; only 1.7%<sup id="cite_ref-177" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-177">&#91;168&#93;</a></sup> of the land is arable, and most food has to be imported. Some tobacco is grown locally. The principal livestock activity is domestic sheep raising. Manufacturing output consists mainly of cigarettes, cigars, and furniture. Andorra's natural resources include hydroelectric power, mineral water, timber, iron ore, and lead.<sup id="cite_ref-cia_5-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-cia-5">&#91;3&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>Andorra is not a member of the European Union, but enjoys a <a href="/wiki/Andorra%E2%80%93European_Union_relations" title="Andorra–European Union relations">special relationship</a> with it, such as being treated as an EU member for trade in manufactured goods (no tariffs) and as a non-EU member for agricultural products. Andorra lacked a currency of its own and used both the <a href="/wiki/French_franc" title="French franc">French franc</a> and the <a href="/wiki/Spanish_peseta" title="Spanish peseta">Spanish peseta</a> in banking transactions until 31 December 1999, when both currencies were replaced by the EU's single currency, the euro. Coins and notes of both the franc and the peseta remained legal tender in Andorra until 31 December 2002. Andorra negotiated to issue its own euro coins, beginning in 2014.
</p><p>Andorra has historically had one of the world's lowest unemployment rates. In 2019 it stood at 2%.<sup id="cite_ref-178" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-178">&#91;169&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>On 31 May 2013, it was announced that Andorra intended to legislate for the introduction of an income tax by the end of June, against a background of increasing dissatisfaction with the existence of tax havens among EU members.<sup id="cite_ref-179" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-179">&#91;170&#93;</a></sup> The announcement was made following a meeting in Paris between the Prime Minister <a href="/wiki/Antoni_Mart%C3%AD" title="Antoni Martí">Antoni Martí</a> and the French President and Prince of Andorra <a href="/wiki/Fran%C3%A7ois_Hollande" title="François Hollande">François Hollande</a>. Hollande welcomed the move as part of a process of Andorra "bringing its taxation in line with international standards".<sup id="cite_ref-180" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-180">&#91;171&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>By the mid-2010s, the financial system comprised five banking groups,<sup id="cite_ref-181" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-181">&#91;172&#93;</a></sup> one specialised credit entity, eight investment undertaking management entities, three asset management companies, and 29 insurance companies, 14 of which are branches of foreign insurance companies authorised to operate in the principality.<sup id="cite_ref-fs2013_174-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-fs2013-174">&#91;165&#93;</a></sup> The last mergers between banks took place in 2022, bringing the Andorran financial sector to currently have 3 active banking groups.<sup id="cite_ref-182" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-182">&#91;173&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Demographics">Demographics</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Andorra&amp;action=edit&amp;section=24" title="Edit section: Demographics">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:222px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Els_Cortals_d%27Encamp_-_1.jpg" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/17/Els_Cortals_d%27Encamp_-_1.jpg/220px-Els_Cortals_d%27Encamp_-_1.jpg" decoding="async" width="220" height="144" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/17/Els_Cortals_d%27Encamp_-_1.jpg/330px-Els_Cortals_d%27Encamp_-_1.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/17/Els_Cortals_d%27Encamp_-_1.jpg/440px-Els_Cortals_d%27Encamp_-_1.jpg 2x" data-file-width="3672" data-file-height="2410" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Els_Cortals_d%27Encamp_-_1.jpg" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>The town of <a href="/wiki/Encamp" title="Encamp">Encamp</a>, as seen from the Vall dels Cortals</div></div></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"/><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/Demographics_of_Andorra" title="Demographics of Andorra">Demographics of Andorra</a></div>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Population">Population</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Andorra&amp;action=edit&amp;section=25" title="Edit section: Population">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<table class="toccolours" style="width:15em;border-top-width:0;border-spacing: 0;float:right;clear:right;margin:0.5em 0 1em 0.5em;"><caption style="border-top:1px #aaa solid;border-left:1px #aaa style="text-align:right;padding:1px">6,176</td><td style="text-align:right;padding:1px">—&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;</td></tr><tr><th style="text-align:center;padding:1px">1960</th><td style="text-align:right;padding:1px">8,392</td><td style="text-align:right;padding:1px">+3.11%</td></tr><tr><th style="text-align:center;padding:1px">1970</th><td style="text-align:right;padding:1px">19,545</td><td style="text-align:right;padding:1px">+8.82%</td></tr><tr><th style="text-align:center;padding:1px">1980</th><td style="text-align:right;padding:1px">35,460</td><td style="text-align:right;padding:1px">+6.14%</td></tr><tr><th style="text-align:center;padding:1px">1990</th><td style="text-align:right;padding:1px">54,507</td><td style="text-align:right;padding:1px">+4.39%</td></tr><tr><th style="text-align:center;padding:1px">2000</th><td style="text-align:right;padding:1px">65,844</td><td style="text-align:right;padding:1px">+1.91%</td></tr><tr><th style="text-align:center;padding:1px">2010</th><td Departament d'Estadística d'Andorra<sup id="cite_ref-183" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-183">&#91;174&#93;</a></sup></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>The population of Andorra is estimated at 79,034 (2021).<sup id="cite_ref-UN_WPP_23-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-UN_WPP-23">&#91;15&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-UN_WPP_2022_24-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-UN_WPP_2022-24">&#91;16&#93;</a></sup> The <a href="/wiki/Andorran_people" class="mw-redirect" title="Andorran people">Andorrans</a> are a <a href="/wiki/Romance-speaking_world" title="Romance-speaking world">Romance</a> <a href="/wiki/Ethnic_group" title="Ethnic group">ethnic group</a> of originally <a href="/wiki/Catalans" title="Catalans">Catalan</a> descent.<sup id="cite_ref-Minahan_25-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Minahan-25">&#91;17&#93;</a></sup> The population has grown from 5,000 in 1900.
</p><p>Two-thirds of residents lack Andorran nationality and do not have the right to vote in communal elections. Moreover, they are not allowed to be elected as prime minister<sup id="cite_ref-184" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-184">&#91;175&#93;</a></sup> or to own more than 33% of the capital stock of a privately held company.<sup id="cite_ref-185" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-185">&#91;176&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-186" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-186">&#91;177&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-187" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-187">&#91;178&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-188" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-188">&#91;179&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Languages">Languages</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Andorra&amp;action=edit&amp;section=26" title="Edit section: Languages">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"/><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/Languages_of_Andorra" title="Languages of Andorra">Languages of Andorra</a></div>
<p>According to mother tongue percentage statistics by the Andorran Government released in 2018&#160;:
</p>
<div class="PieChartTemplate thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:202px">
<div class="mw-no-invert" style="background-color:white;margin:auto;position:relative;width:200px;height:200px;overflow:hidden;border-radius:100px;border:1px solid black;transform:scaleX(-1)rotate(-90deg)">
<div style="border:solid transparent;background-color: initial;position:absolute;width:100px;line-height:0;left:0; top:0; border-width:0 200px 200px 0; border-color:black"></div><div style="border:solid transparent;background-color: initial;position:absolute;width:100px;line-height:0;left:0; top:0; border-width:0 200px 200px 0; border-color:blue"></div><div style="border:solid transparent;background-color: initial;position:absolute;width:100px;line-height:0;left:100px; top:100px; border-width:100px 0 0 389.47428549299px; border-left-color:green"></div>
<div style="position:absolute;line-height:0;border-style:solid;left:0;top:0;border-width:0 200px 100px 0;border-color:green"></div>
<div style="position:absolute;line-height:0;border-style:solid;left:0;top:0;border-width:0 100px 200px 0;border-color:green"></div><div style="border:solid transparent;background-color: initial;position:absolute;width:100px;line-height:0;left:100px; top:100px; border-width:100px 0 0 25.00696627359px; border-left-color:orange"></div>
<div style="position:absolute;line-height:0;border-style:solid;left:0;top:0;border-width:0 200px 100px 0;border-color:orange"></div>
<div style="position:absolute;line-height:0;border-style:solid;left:0;top:0;border-width:0 100px 200px 0;border-color:orange"></div><div style="border:solid transparent;background-color: initial;position:absolute;width:100px;line-height:0;right:100px; top:0; border-width:0 219.63310880999px 100px 0; border-right-color:yellow"></div>
<div style="position:absolute;line-height:0;border-style:solid;right:0;top:0;border-width:0 100px 100px 0;border-color:yellow"></div>
</div>
<div class="thumbcaption">
<p>Mother tongue <sup id="cite_ref-189" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-189">&#91;180&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-190" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-190">&#91;181&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r981673959">.mw-parser-output .legend{page-break-inside:avoid;break-inside:avoid-column}.mw-parser-output .legend-color{display:inline-block;min-width:1.25em;height:1.25em;line-height:1.25;margin:1px 0;text-align:center;border:1px solid black;background-color:transparent;color:black}.mw-parser-output .legend-text{}</style><div class="legend"><span class="legend-color mw-no-invert" style="background-color:yellow; color:black;">&#160;</span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Spanish_language" title="Spanish language">Spanish</a> (43.2%)</div><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r981673959"/><div class="legend"><span class="legend-color mw-no-invert" style="background-color:orange; color:black;">&#160;</span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Catalan_language" title="Catalan language">Catalan</a> (35.7%)</div><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r981673959"/><div class="legend"><span class="legend-color mw-no-invert" style="background-color:green; color:white;">&#160;</span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Portuguese_language" title="Portuguese language">Portuguese</a> (17.1%)</div><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r981673959"/><div class="legend"><span class="legend-color mw-no-invert" style="background-color:blue; color:white;">&#160;</span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/French_language" title="French language">French</a> (8.9%)</div><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r981673959"/><div class="legend"><span class="legend-color mw-no-invert" style="background-color:black; color:white;">&#160;</span>&#160;other (5%)</div>
</div>
</div></div>
<p>The historic and <a href="/wiki/Official_language" title="Official language">official language</a> is <a href="/wiki/Catalan_language" title="Catalan language">Catalan</a>, a <a href="/wiki/Romance_language" class="mw-redirect" title="Romance language">Romance language</a>. The Andorran government encourages the use of Catalan. It funds a Commission for Catalan <a href="/wiki/Toponymy" title="Toponymy">Toponymy</a> in Andorra (Catalan: <span title="Catalan-language text"><i lang="ca">Comissió de Toponímia d'Andorra</i></span>), and provides free Catalan classes to assist immigrants. Andorran television and radio stations use Catalan.
</p><p>Because of immigration, historical links, and close geographic proximity, Spanish, Portuguese and French are commonly spoken. Most Andorran residents can speak one or more of these, in addition to Catalan. English is less commonly spoken among the general population, though it is understood to varying degrees in the major tourist resorts. Andorra is one of only four European countries (together with France, <a href="/wiki/Monaco" title="Monaco">Monaco</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Turkey" title="Turkey">Turkey</a>)<sup id="cite_ref-191" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-191">&#91;182&#93;</a></sup> that have never signed the <a href="/wiki/Framework_Convention_for_the_Protection_of_National_Minorities" title="Framework Convention for the Protection of National Minorities">Council of Europe Framework Convention on National Minorities</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-192" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-192">&#91;183&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Religion">Religion</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Andorra&amp;action=edit&amp;section=27" title="Edit section: Religion">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div class="PieChartTemplate thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:202px">
<div class="mw-no-invert" style="background-color:white;margin:auto;position:relative;width:200px;height:200px;overflow:hidden;border-radius:100px;border:1px solid black;transform:scaleX(-1)rotate(-90deg)">
<div style="border:solid transparent;background-color: initial;position:absolute;width:100px;line-height:0;left:0; top:0; border-width:0 200px 200px 0; border-color:gray"></div><div style="border:solid transparent;background-color: initial;position:absolute;width:100px;line-height:0;left:100px; top:100px; border-width:100px 0 0 109.20735798549px; border-left-color:yellow"></div>
<div style="position:absolute;line-height:0;border-style:solid;left:0;top:0;border-width:0 200px 100px 0;border-color:yellow"></div>
<div style="position:absolute;line-height:0;border-style:solid;left:0;top:0;border-width:0 100px 200px 0;border-color:yellow"></div>
</div>
<div class="thumbcaption">
<p>Religion of andorra
</p>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r981673959"/><div class="legend"><span class="legend-color mw-no-invert" style="background-color:yellow; color:black;">&#160;</span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Catholic_Church" title="Catholic Church">Catholic</a> (88.2%)</div><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r981673959"/><div class="legend"><span class="legend-color mw-no-invert" style="background-color:gray; color:black;">&#160;</span>&#160;other (11.8%)</div>
</div>
</div></div> 
<p>The population of Andorra is predominantly (88.2%) Catholic.<sup id="cite_ref-193" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-193">&#91;184&#93;</a></sup> Their patron saint is <a href="/wiki/Our_Lady_of_Meritxell" title="Our Lady of Meritxell">Our Lady of Meritxell</a>. There are also members of various Protestant denominations. There are also small numbers of <a href="/wiki/Islam_in_Andorra" title="Islam in Andorra">Muslims</a>,<sup id="cite_ref-194" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-194">&#91;185&#93;</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Hinduism" title="Hinduism">Hindus</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Bah%C3%A1%27%C3%AD_Faith_in_Andorra" class="mw-redirect" title="Bahá&#39;í Faith in Andorra">Bahá'ís</a>,<sup id="cite_ref-ARDA05_195-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-ARDA05-195">&#91;186&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-Wolfram_196-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Wolfram-196">&#91;187&#93;</a></sup> and roughly 100 Jews.<sup id="cite_ref-197" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-197">&#91;188&#93;</a></sup> (See <a href="/wiki/History_of_the_Jews_in_Andorra" title="History of the Jews in Andorra">History of the Jews in Andorra</a>.)
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Largest_cities">Largest cities</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Andorra&amp;action=edit&amp;section=28" title="Edit section: Largest cities">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
